//! Column admission, maintained fields and authority/identity/delivery dependencies.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.001#2 — the binder and raw-schema admission share this walk.
use super::*;

pub const GROUP: &str = "$parent";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ColumnOwner {
    pub table: String,
    pub column: String,
}

impl ColumnOwner {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { format!("{}.{}", self.table, self.column) }
    // Bounded labels also keep a first contribution independent of parent state.
    pub fn target(&self) -> String { format!("$column:{}", digest(&(&self.table, &self.column))) }
}

impl Schema {
    pub(crate) fn private_owner(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&ColumnOwner> {
        // Privacy also holds before admission: a duplicate ordinary declaration
        // must never shadow a private one in a raw schema.
        self.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.target == name).find_map(|m| m.private.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn visible(&self, name: &str) -> bool { self.private_owner(name).is_none() && !self.is_recount_work(name) }

    pub fn visible_tables(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&String, &Table)> {
        self.tables.iter().filter(|(name, _)| self.visible(name))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — identity remains on Table, which image-retention compares.
    pub fn public_projection(&self) -> Self {
        let mut schema = self.clone();
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { schema.backfills.clear(); schema.downfills.clear(); schema.table_renames.clear(); schema.asset_renames.clear(); schema.extractions.clear(); }
        // Inspection provenance is catalog side metadata, never device input.
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { schema.authored_indexes = Default::default(); }
        schema.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
        schema.receipt_dependencies.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.source_reservations.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.tables.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.maintains.retain(|m| self.visible(&m.target));
        schema
    }

    pub fn refuse_private(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.is_recount_work(name) { return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_VIEW_KEY",message:"recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()}); }
        if let Some(owner) = self.private_owner(name) {
            return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",
                message: format!("{} is maintained by the engine; read the parent row or write the source rows (guide maintains)", owner.label()) });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A projected device schema deliberately has metadata without private maintains.
    /// Authoritative engines must have exactly one derivation for every owned field.
    pub fn validate_column_owners(&self) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        for (name,table) in self.visible_tables() {
            for column in table.maintained.keys() {
                if self.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o|&o.table == name && &o.column == column)).count() != 1 {
                    return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{name}.{column} needs exactly one engine derivation; declare the column aggregate (guide maintains)")));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Maintain {
    pub fn group_fields(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        if self.private.is_some() { vec![GROUP.into()] } else { self.by.clone() }
    }

    pub fn derivation_identity(&self) -> String { digest(&(&self.kind, &self.source, &self.filter, &self.by)) }
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { self.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| self.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label) }
}

struct Walk<'a> { schema: &'a Schema, issues: Vec<SchemaIssue> }
impl Walk<'_> {
    fn issue(&mut self, path: &str, label: &str) {
        self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message: format!("maintained column {label} is depended on by {path}; use an ordinary column for authority, identity, lifetime or delivery, or a separate maintain for an aggregate (guide maintains)") });
    }
    fn target(&mut self, table: &str, path: &str) {
        if self.schema.is_recount_work(table) {self.issues.push(SchemaIssue {path:path.into(),message:"E_VIEW_KEY: recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()});}
        if let Some(owner) = self.schema.private_owner(table) { self.issue(path, &owner.label()); }
    }
    fn field(&mut self, table: &str, field: &str, path: &str) {
        if let Some(message) = self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, field) {
            self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message });
        }
        if self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.contains_key(field)) { self.issue(path, &format!("{table}.{field}")); }
    }
    fn term(&mut self, table: &str, term: &RuleTerm, path: &str) {
        if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::OldField(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) = term { self.field(table, field, path); }
    }
    fn index(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, path: &str) {
        self.target(table, path);
        if let Some(definition) = self.schema.table(table) && let Some(index) = definition.index(index) {
            for field in definition.maintained.keys().filter(|field| index.uses_column(field)) { self.field(table, field, path); }
            for field in index.columns() {
                if let Some(message) = self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, &field) {
                    self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message });
                }
            }
        }
    }
    fn rule(&mut self, table: &str, rule: &RuleExpr, path: &str) {
        match rule {
            RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny => {},
            RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { self.term(table,a,path); self.term(table,b,path); },
            RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => for item in items { self.rule(table,item,path); },
            RuleExpr::Not(item) => self.rule(table,item,path),
            RuleExpr::Exists { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table: target, index, key, equalities, .. } => {
                self.index(target,index,path);
                for term in key { self.term(table,term,path); }
                for (field,term) in equalities { self.field(target,field,path); self.term(table,term,path); }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Owned timeline rows are written only on the timeline's scoped transition
/// path, even if their stored ownership sets are forged. Mapped application
/// tables stay ordinary parents and sources: the mirror is not a source write,
/// so it never enters a transition, and delivery copies no parent fields.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#33-f3-the-following-recipes-contract-as-a-declaration — timeline writes require their scoped transition path.
pub fn timeline_issues(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut issues = Vec::new();
    for m in &schema.maintains {
        let Some(owner) = &m.private else { continue };
        for (role, table) in [("parent", &owner.table), ("source", &m.source)] {
            if let Some(timeline) = schema.timelines.iter().find(|t| t.owns(table)) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{}.columns.{}", owner.table, owner.column),
                    message: format!("maintained column {} has timeline-owned {role} {table} in {}; the timeline writes its own tables, so use a table it maps, such as its posts, or an ordinary table (guide maintains)", owner.label(), timeline.name) });
            }
        }
    }
    issues
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — raw catalogs must refuse impossible list ranges too.
fn list_bounds_valid(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match kind {
        ColumnType::ListRange { of, min, max } => min <= max && list_bounds_valid(of),
        ColumnType::Optional(inner) | ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. } => list_bounds_valid(inner),
        _ => true,
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.040#13-representation — a raw catalog cannot install a range no int satisfies.
fn int_ranges_valid(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    match kind {
        ColumnType::IntRange { min, max } => crate::schema::int_range_valid(*min, *max),
        ColumnType::Optional(inner) | ColumnType::List { of: inner, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of: inner, .. } => int_ranges_valid(inner),
        _ => true,
    }
}

pub fn validate(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut walk = Walk { schema, issues: Vec::new() };
    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
        for (field, kind) in &table.columns {
            if !list_bounds_valid(kind) {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"),
                    message: "list minimum must not exceed its maximum".into() });
            }
            if !int_ranges_valid(kind) {
                walk.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"),
                    message: "an int range needs -(2^53 - 1) <= min <= max <= 2^53 - 1".into() });
            }
        }
    }
    walk.issues.extend(timeline_issues(schema));
    let mut targets = BTreeSet::new();
    for (position, m) in schema.maintains.iter().enumerate() {
        if !targets.insert(&m.target) {
            let label = schema.private_owner(&m.target).map_or_else(|| m.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label);

    fn parse_on_delete(&mut self, column: &str) -> Fallible<(OnDelete, Site)> {
        let site = self.take("on")?.site;
        self.take("delete")?;
        let action = if self.maybe("restrict") { OnDelete::Restrict }
            else if self.maybe("cascade") { OnDelete::Cascade }
            else if self.maybe("keep") { OnDelete::Keep }
            else if self.at("set") && self.peek_at(1).value == "null" { self.take("set")?; self.take("null")?; OnDelete::SetNull }
            else { return Err(on_delete_error(&site, column)); };
        Ok((action, site))
    }

    fn parse_index(&mut self, unique: bool) -> Fallible<IndexDecl> {
        let site = self.take(if unique { "unique" } else { "by" })?.site;
        let mut explicit = None;
        if self.at_kind(Kind::Word) && self.peek_at(1).value == ":" {
            explicit = Some(self.word()?.value);
            self.take(":")?;
        }
        let mut columns = Vec::new();
        let mut words = None;
        let mut computed = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        loop {
            let component = self.word()?;
            if self.at(".") {
                return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "table index columns cannot be reference paths", "index a column of this table; use recorded paths only on a maintain").guide("indexes"));
            }
            if component.value == "words" && self.at("(") {
                // `words(body, caption)`: one entry per distinct word of the
                // named text columns, in this position.
                if words.is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "an index has one words(..) component", "words(body)"));
                }
                self.take("(")?;
                let mut fields = vec![self.word()?.value];
                while self.maybe(",") {
                    fields.push(self.word()?.value);
                }
                self.take(")")?;
                words = Some((columns.len(), fields));
            } else if matches!(component.value.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year") && self.at("(") {
                self.position -= 1;
                let expression = self.expression(0, &[",", "else", "<newline>"]).map_err(|error| {
                    if error.code == "E_QUARRY_MAINTAIN" { crate::diag::type_error(&error.site, error.message, "day(at), hour(at, 30m), or bucket(at, 2h, 30m); declare at: time on this table").guide("indexes") }
                    else { error }
                })?;
                if computed.insert(component.value.clone(), expression).is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, format!("index repeats {}(..); each time function occurs at most once per index", component.value), "keep one day, hour and bucket component per index; declare another index for another period").guide("indexes"));
                }
                columns.push(component.value);
            } else {
                columns.push(component.value);
            }
            if !self.maybe(",") {
                break;
            }
        }
        let explicit = explicit.or_else(|| unique.then(|| {
            let descriptor = (&columns, &words, computed.iter().map(|(name, value)| (name, crate::print::expression(value))).collect::<Vec<_>>(), true);
            let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&descriptor);
            format!("$intent:by{}:{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>(), &hash[..16])
        }));
        let Some(name) = explicit else {
            let inferred = format!("by{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>());
            return Err(syntax_with(&site, "index declarations require an explicit name", format!("{} {inferred}: {}", if unique { "unique" } else { "by" }, columns.join(", "))));
        };
        if let Some((position, fields)) = &words {
            if unique {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "a words(..) index cannot be unique: a row has one entry per word", format!("by {name}: {}", columns.iter().take(*position).cloned().chain(std::iter::once(format!("words({})", fields.join(", ")))).chain(columns.iter().skip(*position).cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
            }
            if *position == columns.len() {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "words(..) is followed by the columns a search page is ordered by, ending in id", format!("by {name}: {}words({}), at, id", columns.iter().map(|c| format!("{c}, ")).collect::<String>(), fields.join(", "))));
            }
        }
        let refusal = if unique && self.maybe("else") {
            let code = self.word()?.value;
            let message = if self.at_kind(Kind::Str) { Some(self.take_kind(Kind::Str)?.value) } else { None };
            Some((code, message))
        } else { None };
        self.newline()?;
        Ok(IndexDecl { name, columns, unique, refusal, words, computed, site })
    }

    fn parse_maintain(&mut self) -> Fallible<MaintainDecl> {

        let name = self.binding_name()?;
        self.take(":")?;
        let aggregate = self.word()?;
        if aggregate.value != "count" && aggregate.value != "sum" {
            return Err(unsupported(&aggregate.site, &format!("maintain {}", aggregate.value), "use maintain name: count table by columns, or maintain name: sum table of intColumn by columns"));
        }

        || edits.windows(2).any(|pair|pair[0].1>pair[1].0) {
        return Err("unsupported or overlapping retained-table source spans".into());
    }
    edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));
    for (start,end,value) in edits {source.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
    Ok(source)
}
fn join(old:&ast::ScalarType,new:&ast::ScalarType,tables:&BTreeMap<String,(&String,&String,ast::TableDecl)>)->Option<ast::ScalarType> {
    let kind=match (&old.kind,&new.kind) {
        (TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..},TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..})=>{
            let bounds=|k:&TypeKind|match k{TypeKind::IntRange{min,max}=>(min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN),max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX)),_=>(i64::MIN,i64::MAX)};
            let(a,b)=bounds(&old.kind);let(x,y)=bounds(&new.kind);let(min,max)=(a.min(x),b.max(y));
            if min==i64::MIN && max==i64::MAX {TypeKind::Int}else{TypeKind::IntRange{min:(min!=i64::MIN).then_some(min),max:(max!=i64::MAX).then_some(max)}}
        }
        (TypeKind::Image{max:a,once:b},TypeKind::Image{max:x,once:y}) if b==y=>TypeKind::Image{max:(*a).max(*x),once:*b},
        (TypeKind::Video{max:a},TypeKind::Video{max:b})=>TypeKind::Video{max:(*a).max(*b)},
        (TypeKind::File{max:a,types:b},TypeKind::File{max:x,types:y}) if b==y=>TypeKind::File{max:(*a).max(*x),types:b.clone()},
        (TypeKind::Text{min:a,max:b,format:c},TypeKind::Text{min:x,max:y,format:z}) if c==z=>TypeKind::Text{min:Some(a.unwrap_or(0).min(x.unwrap_or(0))).filter(|v|*v>0),max:(*b).max(*y),format:c.clone()},
        (TypeKind::Enum{variants:a},TypeKind::Enum{variants:b})=>{let mut v=a.clone();for x in b {if !v.contains(x){v.push(x.clone());}}TypeKind::Enum{variants:v}},
        (TypeKind::List{item:a,min:b,max:c},TypeKind::List{item:x,min:y,max:z})=>TypeKind::List{item:Box::new(join(a,x,tables)?),min:(*b).min(*y),max:(*c).max(*z)},
        // Only an explicit rename provides nominal identity; structural similarity
        // or independently declared replacement tables cannot justify a bridge.
        (TypeKind::Ref{table:a},TypeKind::Ref{table:b}) if a==b || tables.get(a).is_some_and(|(_,_,t)| t.name==*b)=>TypeKind::Ref{table:a.clone()},
        (a,b) if a==b=>a.clone(),
        // Other conversions require an explicit typed migration. A bridge never
        // guesses a nominal reference, money currency or asset policy.
        _=>return None,
    };
    Some(ast::ScalarType{kind,optional:old.optional||new.optional})

//! Current query dependencies must travel with a selective guarded bridge.
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value};
fn source(text: &str) -> Vec<(String, String)> { vec![("schema.q".into(), text.into())] }
fn rows(bridge: &crate::release_bridge::Bridge, values: &[(&str, i64, i64)], query: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed(values.iter().map(|(id, n, rank)| ("notes".into(), Value::Row([
        ("id".into(), Value::Id((*id).into())), ("n".into(), Value::Int(*n)),
        ("rank".into(), Value::Int(*rank)), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(5)),
    ].into_iter().collect())))).unwrap();
    let result = engine.query(query, Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = result.data else { panic!("expected rows") };
    rows.into_iter().map(|r| match r.field("id").unwrap() { Value::Id(id) => id.clone(), _ => panic!("id") }).collect()
}
#[test]
fn current_index_is_carried_with_its_endpoint() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byN\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable added:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes.contains_key("byN"));
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCount"].columns(), ["count", "id"]);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([("a", 2), ("b", 1)].map(|(id, n)| ("notes".into(), Value::Row([
        ("id".into(), Value::Id(id.into())), ("n".into(), Value::Int(n)),
    ].into_iter().collect())))).unwrap();
    let result = engine.query("ordered", Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = result.data else { panic!("expected rows") };
    assert_eq!(rows.iter().map(|r| r.field("n").unwrap().clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [Value::Int(2), Value::Int(1)]);
}
#[test]
fn same_named_changed_index_uses_current_context() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  rank: int\n  amount: int\n  by byOrder: n, id\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byOrder\n";
    let current = old.replace("amount: int", "amount: int ?").replace("byOrder: n", "byOrder: rank");
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(&current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    assert_eq!(rows(&bridge, &[("a", 1, 9), ("b", 2, 0)], "ordered"), ["b", "a"]);
}
#[test]
fn same_named_word_index_uses_current_field_selection() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  title: text <=40\n  body: text <=40\n  amount: int\n  by bySearch: words(title), id\n  public\n  online only\nquery find(q: text <=40):\n  return notes matching q first 10 by bySearch\n";
    let current = old.replace("amount: int", "amount: int ?").replace("words(title)", "words(body)");
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(&current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["bySearch"].word_component().unwrap().1, ["body"]);
}

#[test]
fn equivalent_unique_key_name_is_not_a_current_index_alias() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  unique byOwner: owner\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byOwner\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  count: int default 7\n  unique byCurrentOwner: owner\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byCurrentOwner\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCurrentOwner"].unique);
}
#[test]
fn declared_plain_column_identity_is_not_an_index_meaning_change() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  by byOwner: owner, id\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byOwner\n";
    let current = old.replace("owner: principal", "customer: principal renamed from owner").replace("byOwner: owner", "byOwner: customer");
    let before = crate::compile(&source(old));
    let after = crate::compile(&source(&current));
    assert!(before.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", before.diagnostics);
    assert!(after.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", after.diagnostics);
    let old_doc = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", old).0;
    let new_doc = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", &current).0;
    assert!(!crate::release_bridge_indexes::current_context_needed(&[old_doc], &[new_doc], &before.schema, &after.schema, &Default::default()));
}

#[test]
fn new_plain_index_uses_declared_predecessor_column_identity() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  owner: principal\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byN\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  n: int ?\n  customer: principal renamed from owner\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, customer, id\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCount"].columns(), ["count", "owner", "id"]);
}

#[test]
fn plain_index_transport_keeps_index_label_and_business_literals() {
    let old = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  online only\n").0;
    let raw = "  unique customer: customer else TAKEN 'customer stays customer'";
    let text = format!("table notes:\n  customer: principal renamed from owner\n{raw}\n  online only\n");
    let current = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", &text).0;
    let next = &current.tables[0];
    let result = crate::release_bridge_indexes::plain_declaration("schema.q", raw, &next.indexes[0], &old.tables[0], next);
    assert_eq!(result, "  unique customer: owner else TAKEN 'customer stays customer'");
}
#[test]
fn plain_column_transport_never_collapses_independent_current_fields() {
    let old = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  online only\n").0;
    let current = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", "table notes:\n  customer: principal renamed from owner\n  owner: principal\n  online only\n").0;
    assert!(crate::release_bridge_indexes::column_names(&old.tables[0], &current.tables[0]).is_none());
}

//! Binding declarations to the core schema: tables, indexes, rules,
//! constraints, maintained aggregates and their generated targets, and the
//! `sync` line with the principal column resolved.

use super::catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo};
use crate::ast::*;
use crate::diag::{Diagnostic, Fallible, Site, ambiguous, name_rewrite, sync_error, type_error, unknown};
use crate::types::{Ty, scalar_to_column};
use snapback4_core::ir::KeyProvenance;
use snapback4_core::schema::{self as core, Audience, Constraint, Horizon, LeavePolicy, RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Rules, Sync, Table};
use snapback4_core::value::Value;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

pub fn bind_schema(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> core::Schema {
    let mut schema = core::Schema::default();
    for info in catalog.tables.values() {
        let table = bind_table(catalog, info, diagnostics);
        for index in &info.indexes {
            if let Some((code, message)) = &index.refusal {
                match super::program::validate_code(&index.site, code) {
                    Ok(()) => {
                        schema.unique_refusals.entry(info.decl.name.clone()).or_default().insert(index.name.clone(), core::UniqueRefusal {
                            code: code.clone(), message: message.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| format!("the program refused with {code}")),
                        });
                    }
                    Err(diagnostic) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic),
                }
            }
        }
        schema.tables.insert(info.decl.name.clone(), table);
    }
    // Identity is semantic, independent of source locations and index planning order.
    for name in schema.through_order() {
        let Some(t) = schema.tables[&name].through.clone() else { continue; };
        let parent=&schema.tables[&t.parent];

}


#[test]
fn guarded_current_view_explicitly_pauses_a_removed_write() {
    let old="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation repair():\n  return insert docs {owner:'old-operator'}\nquery all():\n  return docs first 10 by .id\n";
    let current="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation currentWrite():\n  return insert docs {owner:viewer}\nquery all():\n  return docs first 10 by .id\ninvariant docs.read only true\n";
    let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current.into())]);
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    assert!(bridge.changes.iter().any(|change|change.contains("repair")&&change.contains("E_OP")),"{:?}",bridge.changes);
    let bound=compile(&bridge.sources);assert!(!bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name=="repair"));
    assert!(bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name=="all"));
}


#[test]
fn non_intent_alias_replaced_by_a_program_keeps_its_current_guard() {
    let old="table docs:\n  amount: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation create():\n  return insert docs {amount:1}\nalias protected = create\n";
    let current=old.replace("  amount: int","  amount: int ?").replace("alias protected = create\n","")+"mutation protected():\n  require viewer = 'service:ops' else OPS\n  return insert docs {amount:1}\n";
    let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current)]);assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound=compile(&bridge.sources);let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::memory(bound.schema,bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program)).unwrap();
    for(name,refused)in [("protected",true),("create",false)] {
        let out=engine.mutate(name,None,snapback4_core::Context::new("alice",Default::default())).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(matches!(out.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}),refused,"{name}: {out:?}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn non_intent_guard_transport_cannot_merge_old_input_positions() {
    let old="table docs:\n  amount: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation create(owner: principal, actor: principal):\n  return insert docs {amount:1}\n";
    let current=old.replace("  amount: int","  amount: int ?")
        .replace("create(owner: principal, actor: principal)","create(actor: principal alias owner)")
        .replace("  return insert", "  require actor = 'service:ops' else OPS\n  return insert");
    assert!(compile(&[("schema.q".into(),current.clone())]).diagnostics.is_empty());
    let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current)]);
    assert!(!bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"old owner and actor cannot collapse behind the copied current guard");
}

#[test]
fn guarded_current_view_follows_a_declared_required_column_identity() {
    let old="table docs:\n  price: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nquery all():\n  return docs first 10 by .id\n";
    let current=old.replace("  price: int","  quotedPrice: int renamed from price")+"invariant docs.read only true\n";
    let sources=vec![("schema.q".into(),current)];let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&sources);
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);assert_eq!(bridge.sources,sources);
    assert!(bridge.changes.iter().any(|line|line.contains("docs.price")&&line.contains("docs.quotedPrice")),"{:?}",bridge.changes);
}

#[test]
fn non_intent_query_filter_keeps_the_current_execution_context() {
    let old="table docs:\n  amount: int\n  owner: principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\nquery list():\n  return docs first 10 by .id\n";
    let current=old.replace("amount: int","amount: int ?").replace("return docs first", "return docs where .owner = viewer first");
    let sources=vec![("schema.q".into(),current)];
    assert!(compile(&sources).diagnostics.is_empty());
    let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&sources);
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    assert_eq!(bridge.sources,sources,"restoring an unfiltered query would disclose other viewers' rows");
}

#[test]
fn non_intent_guard_bindings_keep_the_current_statement_order() {
    let old="table docs:\n  amount: int\n  read <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation change(doc: docs):\n  before = docs[doc]\n  update docs[doc] {amount:1}\n  require before.amount = 0 else CLOSED\n";
    let current=old.replace("amount: int","amount: int ?").replace("  before = docs[doc]\n  update docs[doc] {amount:1}","  update docs[doc] {amount:1}\n  before = docs[doc]");
    let sources=vec![("schema.q".into(),current)];
    let bound=compile(&sources);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bound.diagnostics);
    let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&sources);
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    assert_eq!(bridge.sources,sources,"identical last textual bindings cannot authorize restoring an earlier read before a guarded write");
}

#[test]
fn a_named_shape_repair_cannot_restore_a_constant_true_guard() {
    let old="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  amount: int\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nshape access = { allowed: true }\nmutation create():\n  return insert docs {owner:viewer,amount:0}\nmutation change(id: docs):\n  row = docs[id] ! else MISSING\n  gate = row access\n  require gate.allowed else FORBIDDEN\n  update docs[id] {amount:1}\n";
    let current=old.replace("amount: int","amount: int ?").replace("allowed: true","allowed: .owner = viewer");
    let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current)]);
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound=compile(&bridge.sources);let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::memory(bound.schema,bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program)).unwrap();
    let created=engine.mutate("create",None,snapback4_core::Context::new("alice",Default::default())).unwrap();
    let snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=created.state else{panic!("{created:?}")};
    let args=std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),result.field("id").unwrap().clone())]);
    for(who,refused)in [("bob",true),("alice",false)] {
        let out=engine.mutate("change",None,snapback4_core::Context::new(who,args.clone())).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(matches!(out.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}),refused,"{who}: {out:?}");
    }
}

fn changed_existing_column_authority_selects_the_guarded_current_fallback() {
    let old="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  amount: int\n  legacy: int ?\n  secret: text <=40 read <- .owner = viewer\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation create():\n  return insert docs {owner:viewer,amount:1,secret:'private'}\n";
    let current=old.replace("amount: int","amount: int ?").replace("  legacy: int ?\n","").replace("read <- .owner = viewer","read <- deny");
    let sources=vec![("schema.q".into(),current)];
    assert!(compile(&sources).diagnostics.is_empty());
    let bridge=release_bridge::loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&sources);
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bridge.diagnostics);
    assert_eq!(bridge.sources,sources,"a valid current guarded view must be selected before late CLI authority admission");

//! Asset/reference renames transport cells, homes and lifetime evidence.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — asset identity is preserved; no upload, re-claim or generated placeholder.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
type Maps=BTreeMap<String,BTreeMap<String,String>>;
fn refusal(message:impl Into<String>)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:message.into()}}
fn replace_keys<T>(fields:BTreeMap<String,T>,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->BTreeMap<String,T> {fields.into_iter().map(|(name,value)|(map.get(&name).cloned().unwrap_or(name),value)).collect()}
fn table(table:&mut Table,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){
    table.columns=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.columns),map);
    table.defaults=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.defaults),map);
    table.column_reads=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.column_reads),map);
    table.on_delete=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.on_delete),map);
    table.written_by=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.written_by),map);
    fn term(t:&mut crate::schema::RuleTerm,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){use crate::schema::RuleTerm::*;if let Field(c)|OldField(c)|NextField(c)|Reference{column:c,..}|ReferenceChain{column:c,..}=t {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}}}
    fn rule(r:&mut crate::schema::RuleExpr,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;match r {Eq(a,b)|Ne(a,b)|Compare(_,a,b)=>{term(a,map);term(b,map)},And(xs)|Or(xs)=>for x in xs{rule(x,map)},Not(r)=>rule(r,map),Exists{key,equalities,..}|Created{key,equalities,..}=>{for t in key{term(t,map)}for(_,t)in equalities{term(t,map)}},_=>()}}
    for r in [&mut table.rules.read,&mut table.rules.insert,&mut table.rules.update,&mut table.rules.delete]{rule(r,map)}
    for r in table.column_reads.values_mut(){rule(r,map)}
    for index in table.indexes.values_mut(){for component in &mut index.components {if let crate::schema::IndexComponent::Column(c)=component {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}}}}
    if let Some(sync)=&mut table.sync {
        for audience in std::iter::once(&mut sync.audience).chain(sync.also.iter_mut().map(|a|&mut a.audience)) {
            match audience {crate::schema::Audience::Column(c)=>{if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}},crate::schema::Audience::Target{prefix,..}=>for c in prefix {if let Some(new)=map.get(c){*c=new.clone();}},_=>()}
        }
    }
    for c in &mut table.constraints {match c{
        crate::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields}|crate::schema::Constraint::Cap{by:fields,..}=>for f in fields{if let Some(new)=map.get(f){*f=new.clone();}},
        crate::schema::Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..}=>for t in key{term(t,map)},
        _=>(),
    }}
}
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — comparison uses the same typed identity codec as activation.
impl Schema {
    /// Compare schema authority using declared physical column identities.
    pub fn with_column_names(&self,maps:&BTreeMap<String,BTreeMap<String,String>>)->Self{schema(self.clone(),maps)}
}
pub(super) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,maps:&Maps)->Schema {
    for(name,map)in maps {if let Some(t)=schema.tables.get_mut(name){table(t,map);}}
    for e in &mut schema.extractions {
        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.child){e.reference=map.get(&e.reference).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||e.reference.clone());e.fields=std::mem::take(&mut e.fields).into_iter().map(|(dest,source)|(map.get(&dest).cloned().unwrap_or(dest),source)).collect();}
        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.parent){for source in e.fields.values_mut(){if let Some(new)=map.get(source){*source=new.clone();}}}
    }schema
}
pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&BTreeMap<String,i64>,renamed_tables:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Maps,RuntimeError>{
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    if !exists && next.asset_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(Maps::new())}
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _asset_column_renames(t INTEGER NOT NULL,name TEXT NOT NULL,origin TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    let mut maps=Maps::new();
    for (name,current) in &next.tables {
        let Some(t)=catalog.get(name) else {continue};
        let mut aliases:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        for (new,old) in next.asset_renames.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            if old==new || current.columns.contains_key(old) || !current.columns.get(new).is_some_and(asset){return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} requires one predecessor-only source and one asset destination")))}
            let root=aliases.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
            if aliases.get(new).is_some_and(|r|r!=&root){return Err(refusal("asset rename cannot merge independent column lifetimes"))}
            for c in [old,new] {aliases.insert(c.clone(),root.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _asset_column_renames VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,c,root])?;}
        }
        let mut selected=BTreeMap::new();for c in current.columns.keys(){if let Some(root)=aliases.get(c){if selected.insert(root.clone(),c.clone()).is_some(){return Err(refusal("two names for the same asset column cannot be active together"))}}}
        let map:BTreeMap<_,_>=aliases.into_iter().filter_map(|(old,root)|selected.get(&root).filter(|new|**new!=old).map(|new|(old,new.clone()))).collect();
        for(old,new)in &map {
            let prior:Option<String>=conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
            if let Some(prior)=prior {
                let prior:Schema=serde_json::from_str(&prior).map_err(|e|refusal(e.to_string()))?;
                let prior=prior.with_table_names(renamed_tables)?;
                if let Some(before)=prior.table(name).filter(|t|t.columns.contains_key(old)){
                    let source=&before.columns[old];let destination=&current.columns[new];
                    if reference(source)||reference(destination){
                        if source!=destination||before.on_delete.get(old)!=current.on_delete.get(new)||before.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*old)||current.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*new){return Err(refusal("reference rename requires identical nominal type, deletion policy and uncaptured ownership in both directions"))}
                    }
                }
            }
            for rows in ["r","_dormant_rows","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {
                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
                let a=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(old).unwrap());let b=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(new).unwrap());
                let conflict:bool=conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {rows} WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL AND json_type(data,?3) IS NOT NULL AND json_extract(data,?2)!=json_extract(data,?3))"),params![t,a,b],|r|r.get(0))?;
                if conflict{return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} has a conflicting physical destination")))}
                conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET data=json_remove(json_set(data,?3,json_extract(data,?2)),?2) WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL"),params![t,a,b])?;
            }
            conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_column=?3 WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_column=?2",params![name,old,new])?;
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_refs")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_values")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_columns")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_columns SET column_name=?3 WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![name,old,new])?;}
        }
        if !map.is_empty(){
            for rows in ["_dormant_narrow_rows","_dormant_tables"] {
                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
                let condition=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{"table_name=?1"}else{"t=?1"};
                let value=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{rusqlite::types::Value::Text(name.clone())}else{rusqlite::types::Value::Integer(*t)};
                let definitions:Vec<String>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT DISTINCT definition FROM {rows} WHERE {condition}"))?.query_map([&value],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
                for text in definitions {let mut definition:Table=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;table(&mut definition,&map);conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET definition=?2 WHERE {condition} AND definition=?3"),params![value,canonical_json(&definition),text])?;}
            }
            maps.insert(name.clone(),map);
        }
    }
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[super::table_renames::VERSION])?;
    Ok(maps)
}
fn exists_table(conn:&Connection,name:&str)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get(0))?)}
